\displaystyle\Pi_{42}\leq

\displaystyle K\big(n^{-\frac{1}{2}}+n^{-\frac{\bar{p}}{4}}+n^{-\frac{3}{4}}\big)E\int_{0}^{t}\big(1+|x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n}|^{\bar{p}}+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}|x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{k,N,n}|^{\bar{p}}\big)ds

\displaystyle+KE\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Z}n^{-\frac{1}{4}}\big|(\widehat{\gamma})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(s)},\bar{z}\big)\big|^{\bar{p}}\nu(d\bar{z})ds

\displaystyle+KE\Big\{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Z}n^{\frac{\bar{p}-2}{8}}\sum_{\mathfrak{q}=1}^{2}\big(E_{\kappa_{n}(s)}\big|(\widehat{\gamma}_{\mathfrak{q}})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(s)},\bar{z}\big)\big|^{2}\big)^{\frac{\bar{p}}{2}}\nu(d\bar{z})ds\Big\}

\displaystyle+KE\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Z}|x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n}|^{\bar{p}-2}n^{-\frac{1}{4}}\big|(\widehat{\gamma})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(s)},\bar{z}\big)\big|^{2}\nu(d\bar{z})ds

\displaystyle+KE\Big\{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Z}|x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n}|^{\bar{p}-2}n^{\frac{1}{4}}\sum_{\mathfrak{q}=1}^{2}\big(E_{\kappa_{n}(s)}\big|(\widehat{\gamma}_{\mathfrak{q}})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(s)},\bar{z}\big)\big|\big)^{2}\nu(d\bar{z})ds\Big\}

\displaystyle+KE\Big\{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Z}n^{\frac{1}{4(\bar{p}-1)}}\Big(\sum_{\mathfrak{q}=1}^{2}E_{\kappa_{n}(s)}\big|(\widehat{\gamma}_{\mathfrak{q}})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(s)},\bar{z}\big)\big|^{\bar{p}-1}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+E_{\kappa_{n}(s)}|x_{s}^{i,N,n}-x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n}|^{\bar{p}-1}\Big)^{\frac{\bar{p}}{\bar{p}-1}}\nu(d\bar{z})ds\Big\}

\displaystyle\quad+KE\Big\{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Z}n^{\frac{\bar{p}-1}{4}}\sum_{\mathfrak{q}=1}^{2}\big(E_{\kappa_{n}(s)}\big|(\widehat{\gamma}_{\mathfrak{q}})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(s)},\bar{z}\big)\big|\big)^{\bar{p}}\nu(d\bar{z})ds\Big\}

\displaystyle+KE\Big\{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Z}n^{\frac{1}{4(\bar{p}-1)}}\sum_{\mathfrak{q}=1}^{2}\big(E_{\kappa_{n}(s)}\big|(\widehat{\gamma}_{\mathfrak{q}})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(s)},\bar{z}\big)\big|^{\bar{p}-1}\big)^{\frac{\bar{p}}{\bar{p}-1}}\nu(d\bar{z})ds\Big\}

which on using Assumption

, Lemmas

,

and Hölder’s inequality give

\displaystyle\Pi_{42}\leq K\Big\{1+n^{\frac{\bar{p}-2}{8}}(n^{-\frac{\bar{p}}{4}}+n^{-\frac{\bar{p}}{2}+\frac{1}{2}})+n^{\frac{1}{4(\bar{p}-1)}}\big(n^{-\frac{\bar{p}}{4}}+n^{-\frac{\bar{p}}{(\bar{p}-1)}+\frac{1}{2}}+n^{-\frac{3\bar{p}}{4(\bar{p}-1)}}\big)\Big\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times E\int_{0}^{t}\big(1+|x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n}|^{\bar{p}}+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}|x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{k,N,n}|^{\bar{p}}\big)ds

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+KE\Big\{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Z}|x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n}|^{\bar{p}-2}n^{\frac{1}{4}}\sum_{\mathfrak{q}=1}^{2}E_{\kappa_{n}(s)}\big|(\widehat{\gamma}_{\mathfrak{q}})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(s)},\bar{z}\big)\big|^{2}\nu(d\bar{z})ds\Big\}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+KE\Big\{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Z}n^{\frac{\bar{p}-1}{4}}\sum_{\mathfrak{q}=1}^{2}\big(E_{\kappa_{n}(s)}\big|(\widehat{\gamma}_{\mathfrak{q}})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(s)},\bar{z}\big)\big|^{2}\big)^{\frac{\bar{p}}{2}}\nu(d\bar{z})ds\Big\}

\displaystyle\quad\,\,\leq K\Big\{1+n^{\frac{1}{4}}n^{-\frac{1}{2}}+n^{\frac{\bar{p}-1}{4}}(n^{-\frac{\bar{p}}{4}}+n^{-\frac{\bar{p}}{2}+\frac{1}{2}})\Big\}E\int_{0}^{t}\big(1+|x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n}|^{\bar{p}}+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}|x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{k,N,n}|^{\bar{p}}\big)ds

(28)

\displaystyle\quad\,\,\leq K\int_{0}^{t}\big(1+\sup_{i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\sup_{r\in[0,s]}E|x_{r}^{i,N,n}|^{\bar{p}}\big)ds

for any
t\in[0,T]
and
i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
.
Further, by using Equations (

) and (
28
) in Equation (

) one gets

(29)

\displaystyle\Pi_{4}\leq K\int_{0}^{t}\big(1+\sup_{i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\sup_{r\in[0,s]}E|x_{r}^{i,N,n}|^{\bar{p}}\big)ds

for any
t\in[0,T]
and

.
Then, by using Equations (

), (

), (

), (

) and (

) in Equation (

) one gets for any

and

\displaystyle\sup_{i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\sup_{r\in[0,t]}E|x_{r}^{i,N,n}|^{\bar{p}}\leq E|x_{0}^{i}|^{\bar{p}}+K+K\int_{0}^{t}\sup_{i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\sup_{r\in[0,s]}E|x_{r}^{i,N,n}|^{\bar{p}}ds

which on using Assumption

and Grönwall’s inequality completes the proof.
∎

4.
Rate of Convergence

In this section, we prove the main result stated in Theorem
2.1
.
For this purpose, first one needs to establish auxiliary results given below.

The following corollaries are consequences of Lemma
3.3
.

Corollary 4.1
.

If Assumptions
2.1
,
2.2
and
2.6
to
2.11
hold,
then for
0\leq p_{0}\leq{\bar{p}}/{(\eta+1)}

\displaystyle E|(\widehat{b})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(s)}\big)|^{p_{0}}+E|(\widehat{\sigma})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(s)}\big)|^{p_{0}}+\int_{Z}E|(\widehat{\gamma})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(s)},\bar{z}\big)|^{p_{0}}\nu(d\bar{z})\leq

\displaystyle K

for any
s\in[0,T]
and
i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
where
K>0
is independent of
N
and

.

Corollary 4.2
.

If Assumptions
2.1
,

and

to

hold, then for

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathfrak{q}=1}^{2}E\big|(\widehat{\sigma}_{\mathfrak{q}})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(s)}\big)\big|^{p_{0}}+\sum_{\mathfrak{q}=1}^{2}\int_{Z}E\big|(\widehat{\gamma}_{\mathfrak{q}})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(s)},\bar{z}\big)\big|^{p_{0}}\nu(d\bar{z})\leq

\displaystyle Kn^{-1}

for any

and

is independent of

and

.

.

If Assumptions

,

\displaystyle E|x_{s}^{i,N,n}-x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n}|^{p_{0}}\leq Kn^{-1}

.

For notational simplicity, let us introduce
a
d\times m
matrix
(\widehat{\Lambda})_{tam}^{\displaystyle n}
whose
u\ell
-th element is

\displaystyle(\widehat{\Lambda}^{u\ell})_{tam}^{\displaystyle n}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{x,N,n}\big):=(\widehat{\sigma}^{u\ell})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(s)}\big)+\sum_{\mathfrak{q}=1}^{2}(\widehat{\sigma}_{\mathfrak{q}}^{u\ell})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(s)}\big)

u
-th component is

\displaystyle(\widehat{\Gamma}^{u})_{tam}^{\displaystyle n}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{x,N,n},\bar{z}\big):=(\widehat{\gamma}^{u})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(s)},\bar{z}\big)+\sum_{\mathfrak{q}=1}^{2}(\widehat{\gamma}_{\mathfrak{q}}^{u})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(s)},\bar{z}\big)

for any

,

Now, let us rewrite the Milstein-type scheme (

) as follows

\displaystyle x_{t}^{i,N,n}=x_{0}^{i}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}(\widehat{b})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(s)}\big)ds+\int_{0}^{t}(\widehat{\Lambda})_{tam}^{\displaystyle n}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{x,N,n}\big)dw_{s}^{i}

(30)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Z}(\widehat{\Gamma})_{tam}^{\displaystyle n}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{x,N,n},\bar{z}\big)\tilde{n}_{p}^{i}(ds,d\bar{z})

almost surely for any

and

Lemma 4.1
.

Let Assumptions
2.1
,
2.2
and
2.6
to
2.18
hold with

\bar{p}\geq\max\{2,\eta/8+1\}(\varepsilon+2)(\eta+1)(\eta+2)/{\varepsilon}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

where
\varepsilon\in(0,1)
.
Then,

\displaystyle\int_{Z}E\big|\gamma\big(x_{s}^{i,N,n},\mu_{s}^{x,N,n},z\big)-(\widehat{\Gamma})_{tam}^{\displaystyle n}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{x,N,n},z\big)\big|^{2}\nu(dz)\leq Kn^{-1-\frac{2}{\varepsilon+2}}

for any
s\in[0,T]
and
i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
where
K>0
is a constant independent of
N
and
n
.

Proof.

First, recall the Milstein-type scheme (
30
) and use Itô’s formula in Equation (
69
) (Lemma
2.1
) to get

\displaystyle\gamma^{u}\big(x_{s}^{i,N,n},\mu_{s}^{x,N,n},z\big)=\gamma^{u}\big(x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{x,N,n},z\big)+\int_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{s}\partial_{x}\gamma^{u}\big(x_{r}^{i,N,n},\mu_{r}^{x,N,n},z\big)(\widehat{b})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(x_{\kappa_{n}(r)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(r)}\big)dr

\displaystyle-\int_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{s}\int_{Z}\partial_{x}\gamma^{u}\big(x_{r}^{i,N,n},\mu_{r}^{x,N,n},z\big)(\widehat{\Gamma})_{tam}^{\displaystyle n}\big(\kappa_{n}(r),r,x_{\kappa_{n}(r)}^{i,N,n},\mu_{\kappa_{n}(r)}^{x,N,n},\bar{z}\big)\nu(d\bar{z})dr

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}\int_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{s}\partial_{\mu}\gamma^{u}\big(x_{r}^{i,N,n},\mu_{r}^{x,N,n},x_{r}^{k,N,n},z\big)(\widehat{b})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(x_{\kappa_{n}(r)}^{k,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(r)}\big)dr

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}\int_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{s}\int_{Z}\partial_{\mu}\gamma^{u}\big(x_{r}^{i,N,n},\mu_{r}^{x,N,n},x_{r}^{k,N,n},z\big)(\widehat{\Gamma})_{tam}^{\displaystyle n}(\kappa_{n}(r),r,x_{\kappa_{n}(r)}^{k,N,n},\mu_{\kappa_{n}(r)}^{x,N,n},\bar{z})\nu(d\bar{z})dr

\displaystyle+\sum_{\ell_{1}=1}^{m}\int_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{s}\partial_{x}\gamma^{u}\big(x_{r}^{i,N,n},\mu_{r}^{x,N,n},z\big)(\widehat{\Lambda}^{\ell_{1}})_{tam}^{\displaystyle n}\big(\kappa_{n}(r),r,x_{\kappa_{n}(r)}^{i,N,n},\mu_{\kappa_{n}(r)}^{x,N,n}\big)dw^{i\ell_{1}}_{r}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\ell_{1}=1}^{m}\sum_{k=1}^{N}\int_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{s}\partial_{\mu}\gamma^{u}\big(x_{r}^{i,N,n},\mu_{r}^{x,N,n},x_{r}^{k,N,n},z\big)(\widehat{\Lambda}^{\ell_{1}})_{tam}^{\displaystyle n}\big(\kappa_{n}(r),r,x_{\kappa_{n}(r)}^{k,N,n},\mu_{\kappa_{n}(r)}^{x,N,n}\big)dw^{k\ell_{1}}_{r}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{s}\tr\big[\partial_{x}^{2}\gamma^{u}\big(x_{r}^{i,N,n},\mu_{r}^{x,N,n},z\big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times(\widehat{\Lambda})_{tam}^{\displaystyle n}\big(\kappa_{n}(r),r,x_{\kappa_{n}(r)}^{i,N,n},\mu_{\kappa_{n}(r)}^{x,N,n}\big)(\widehat{\Lambda}^{*})_{tam}^{{\displaystyle n}}\big(\kappa_{n}(r),r,x_{\kappa_{n}(r)}^{i,N,n},\mu_{\kappa_{n}(r)}^{x,N,n}\big)\big]dr

\displaystyle Kn^{-1-\frac{2}{2+\varepsilon}}+K\int_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{s}\bigg\{\Big[E\int_{Z}\Big\{1+|x_{r}^{i,N,n}|+|x_{\kappa_{n}(r)}^{i,N,n}|+\big|(\widehat{\gamma})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(x_{\kappa_{n}(r)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(r)},\bar{z}\big)\big|

\displaystyle\times\Big[E\int_{Z}\Big\{|x_{r}^{i,N,n}-x_{\kappa_{n}(r)}^{i,N,n}|^{\varepsilon+2}+\sum_{\mathfrak{q}=1}^{2}\big|(\widehat{\gamma}_{\mathfrak{q}})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(\kappa_{n}(r),r,x_{\kappa_{n}(r)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(r)},\bar{z}\big)\big|^{\varepsilon+2}\Big\}\nu(d\bar{z})\Big]^{\frac{2}{\varepsilon+2}}\bigg\}dr

for any

,

.

One can prove the following lemma by using arguments similar to the Lemma

shown above.

Lemma 4.2
.

Let Assumptions
2.1
,
2.2
and
2.6
to

hold
with

.
Then,

\displaystyle E\big|\sigma\big(x_{s}^{i,N,n},\mu_{s}^{x,N,n}\big)-(\widehat{\Lambda})_{tam}^{\displaystyle n}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{x,N,n}\big)\big|^{2}\leq Kn^{-1-\frac{2}{\varepsilon+2}}

for any
s\in[0,T]
and

where

is a constant independent of

and

Recalling the interacting particle system (
3
) and the Milstein-type scheme (

), let us use the following
d\times 1
vector notation whose
u

\displaystyle e_{t}^{iu,N,n}:=x_{t}^{iu,N}-

\displaystyle x_{t}^{iu,N,n}=\int_{0}^{t}\Big\{b^{u}(x_{s}^{iu,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})-(\widehat{b}^{u})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(s)}\big)\Big\}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\Big\{\sigma^{(u)}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})-(\widehat{\Lambda}^{(u)})_{tam}^{\displaystyle n}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{x,N,n}\big)\Big\}dw^{i}_{s}

(45)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Z}\Big\{\gamma^{u}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},\bar{z})-(\widehat{\Gamma}^{u})_{tam}^{\displaystyle n}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{x,N,n},\bar{z}\big)\Big\}\tilde{n}_{p}^{i}(ds,d\bar{z})

for any

,

.

Lemma 4.3
.

Let Assumptions
2.1
to
2.3
and
2.5

to

hold
with

\bar{p}\geq\max\{2,\eta/8+1\}(\varepsilon+2)(\eta+1)(\eta+2)/{\varepsilon}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

\varepsilon\in(0,1)
.
Then,

\displaystyle E\int_{0}^{t}

\displaystyle e_{s}^{i,N,n}\Big\{b\big(x_{s}^{i,N,n},\mu_{s}^{x,N,n}\big)-(\widehat{b})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(s)}\big)\Big\}ds

\displaystyle\leq K\int_{0}^{t}\sup_{i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\sup_{r\in[0,s]}E|e_{r}^{i,N,n}|^{2}ds+Kn^{-1-\frac{2}{\varepsilon+2}}

for any

and

where

is a constant independent of

and

Proof.

First, recall the Milstein-type scheme (

) and use Itô’s formula in Equation (

) (Lemma

Proof of Theorem
2.1
.

Recall Equation (
45
) and use Itô’s formula
[
46
, Theorem 94]
to write

\displaystyle|x_{t}^{i,N}

\displaystyle-x_{t}^{i,N,n}|^{2}=2\int_{0}^{t}(x_{s}^{i,N}-x_{s}^{i,N,n})\Big\{b(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})-(\widehat{b})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(s)}\big)\Big\}ds

\displaystyle+2\int_{0}^{t}(x_{s}^{i,N}-x_{s}^{i,N,n})\Big\{\sigma(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})-(\widehat{\Lambda})_{tam}^{\displaystyle n}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{x,N,n}\big)\Big\}dw^{i}_{s}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\big|\sigma(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})-(\widehat{\Lambda})_{tam}^{\displaystyle n}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{x,N,n}\big)\big|^{2}ds

\displaystyle+2\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Z}(x_{s}^{i,N}-x_{s}^{i,N,n})\Big\{\gamma(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},\bar{z})-(\widehat{\Gamma})_{tam}^{\displaystyle n}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{x,N,n},\bar{z}\big)\Big\}\tilde{n}_{p}^{i}(ds,d\bar{z})

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Z}\Big[\big|x_{s}^{i,N}-x_{s}^{i,N,n}+\gamma(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},\bar{z})-(\widehat{\Gamma})_{tam}^{\displaystyle n}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{x,N,n},\bar{z}\big)\big|^{2}-|x_{s}^{i,N}-x_{s}^{i,N,n}|^{2}

\displaystyle-2(x_{s}^{i,N}-x_{s}^{i,N,n})\Big\{\gamma(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},\bar{z})-(\widehat{\Gamma})_{tam}^{\displaystyle n}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{x,N,n},\bar{z}\big)\Big\}\Big]{n}_{p}^{i}(ds,d\bar{z})

which on taking expectations on both sides and using

|y_{1}+y_{2}|^{2}-|y_{1}|^{2}-2y_{1}y_{2}=|y_{2}|^{2},y_{1},y_{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}

give

\displaystyle E|x_{t}^{i,N}-x_{t}^{i,N,n}|^{2}=2E\int_{0}^{t}(x_{s}^{i,N}-x_{s}^{i,N,n})\Big\{b(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})-(\widehat{b})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(s)}\big)\Big\}ds

\displaystyle+E\int_{0}^{t}\big|\sigma(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})-(\widehat{\Lambda})_{tam}^{\displaystyle n}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{x,N,n}\big)\big|^{2}ds

\displaystyle+E\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Z}\big|\gamma(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},\bar{z})-(\widehat{\Gamma})_{tam}^{\displaystyle n}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{x,N,n},\bar{z}\big)\big|^{2}\nu(d\bar{z})ds

for any
t\in[0,T]
and
i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
.
Further, one uses

|y_{1}+y_{2}|^{2}\leq\alpha|y_{1}|^{2}+\frac{\alpha}{\alpha-1}|y_{2}|^{2},\,y_{1},y_{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}\times m_{1}}

for some
\alpha>1
(obtained due to Young’s inequality and Cauchy–Schwarz inequality) as follows

\displaystyle E|x_{t}^{i,N}-x_{t}^{i,N,n}|^{2}\leq E\int_{0}^{t}\Big[2(x_{s}^{i,N}-x_{s}^{i,N,n})\Big\{b(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})-b(x_{s}^{i,N,n},\mu_{s}^{x,N,n})\Big\}

\displaystyle+\alpha\big|\sigma(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})-\sigma(x_{s}^{i,N,n},\mu_{s}^{x,N,n})\big|^{2}+\alpha\int_{Z}\big|\gamma(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},\bar{z})-\gamma(x_{s}^{i,N,n},\mu_{s}^{x,N,n},\bar{z})\big|^{2}\nu(d\bar{z})\Big]ds

\displaystyle+2E\int_{0}^{t}(x_{s}^{i,N}-x_{s}^{i,N,n})\Big\{b(x_{s}^{i,N,n},\mu_{s}^{x,N,n})-(\widehat{b})_{tam}^{\displaystyle{n}}\big(x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu^{x,N,n}_{\kappa_{n}(s)}\big)\Big\}ds

\displaystyle+\frac{\alpha}{\alpha-1}E\int_{0}^{t}\big|\sigma(x_{s}^{i,N,n},\mu_{s}^{x,N,n})-(\widehat{\Lambda})_{tam}^{\displaystyle n}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{x,N,n}\big)\big|^{2}ds

\displaystyle+\frac{\alpha}{\alpha-1}E\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Z}\big|\gamma(x_{s}^{i,N,n},\mu_{s}^{x,N,n},\bar{z})-(\widehat{\Gamma})_{tam}^{\displaystyle n}\big(\kappa_{n}(s),s,x_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{i,N,n},\mu_{\kappa_{n}(s)}^{x,N,n},\bar{z}\big)\big|^{2}\nu(d\bar{z})ds

which on applying Assumption
2.2
and Lemmas
4.1
to
4.3
give

\displaystyle\sup_{i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\sup_{r\in[0,t]}E|x_{r}^{i,N}-x_{r}^{i,N,n}|^{2}=K\int_{0}^{t}\sup_{i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\sup_{r\in[0,s]}E|x_{r}^{i,N}-x_{r}^{i,N,n}|^{2}ds+Kn^{-1-\frac{2}{\varepsilon+2}}

for any
t\in[0,T]
and
i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
.
Then, one uses Grönwall’s inequality to complete the proof.
∎

Appendix A
Proof of Some Auxiliary Results

In this section, we prove some useful lemmas used in our article.

Proof of Lemma
2.1
.

We recall the intensity
\nu(Z)<\infty
.
As
\{n^{1}_{p}(ds,dz),\ldots,n^{N}_{p}(ds,dz)\}
are
N

i.i.d.
copies of the Poisson random measure
n_{p}(ds,dz)
, the particles
\{x^{1,N},\ldots,x^{N,N}\}
do not jump together and each of them jumps finitely many times in a finite interval.
Let us denote the jump-times of the
i
-th particle
x^{i,N}
by
0=\widetilde{\tau}_{0}^{\,i}<\widetilde{\tau}_{1}^{\,i}<\cdots<\widetilde{\tau}_{\widetilde{m}_{i}}^{\,i}<\cdots
for
i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
.
Moreover, assume that
0=\widetilde{\tau}_{0}<\widetilde{\tau}_{1}<\cdots<\widetilde{\tau}_{\widetilde{m}}<\cdots
represent the combined jump-times of all the
N
particles where
\widetilde{m}=\widetilde{m}_{1}+\cdots+\widetilde{m}_{N}
.
For proceeding further, let us rewrite Equation (
3
) as

\displaystyle x_{t}^{iu,N}=x_{0}^{iu}+\int_{0}^{t}b^{u}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})ds

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Z}\gamma^{u}(x^{i,N}_{s},\mu_{s}^{x,N},z)\nu(dz)ds+\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\int_{0}^{t}\sigma^{u\ell}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})dw_{s}^{i\ell}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Z}\gamma^{u}(x^{i,N}_{s},\mu_{s}^{x,N},z){n}_{p}^{i}(ds,dz)

almost surely for any
t\in[0,T]
,
u\in\{1,\ldots,d\}
and
i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
.
Further, consider the following empirical projection of
F

\displaystyle F^{N}:\mathbb{R}^{d}\times\mathbb{R}^{d\times N}\ni(x,\mathcal{X})\mapsto F\Big(x,\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\delta_{x^{j}}\Big)

where
\mathcal{X}\hskip-1.9919pt:=\hskip-1.9919pt(x^{1},\ldots,x^{N})\hskip-1.70709pt\in\hskip-1.70709pt\mathbb{R}^{d\times N}
.
Also,
\partial_{x}F^{N}:=(\partial_{x_{u}}F^{N})_{u\in\{1,\ldots,d\}}
, and
\partial^{2}_{x}F^{N}:=(\partial_{x_{v}}\partial_{x_{u}}F^{N})_{u,v\in\{1,\ldots,d\}}
are used to denote the gradient and Hessian matrix of
F^{N}
with respect to the first component of
F^{N}
.
Similarly,
\partial_{x^{k}}F^{N}:=(\partial_{x_{u}^{k}}F^{N})_{u\in\{1,\ldots,d\}}
and
\partial^{2}_{x^{k}}F^{N}:=(\partial_{x_{v}^{k}}\partial_{x_{u}^{k}}F^{N})_{u,v\in\{1,\ldots,d\}}
stand for the gradient and Hessian matrix of
F^{N}
with respect to the
k
-th component
x^{k}
of
\mathcal{X}
for
k\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
.
Now, we write

\displaystyle F^{N}(x_{t}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{t}^{N})-

\displaystyle F^{N}(x_{0}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{0}^{N})=\sum_{m=1}^{\infty}\big(F^{N}(x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{N})-F^{N}(x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m-1}\wedge t}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m-1}\wedge t}^{N})\big)

(66)

\displaystyle+\sum_{m=1}^{\infty}\big(F^{N}(x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}^{N})-F^{N}(x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{N})\big)

almost surely where
\mathcal{X}_{t}^{N}:=(x_{t}^{1,N},\ldots,x_{t}^{N,N})
and
\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-=(\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t)-
for any
t\geq 0
.
For the first term on the right side of Equation (
66
), notice that there is no jump in the interval
[\widetilde{\tau}_{m-1}\wedge t,\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t)
.
Thus, one uses the Itô’s formula (see for example, Theorem 94 in
[
46
]
) to obtain the following

\displaystyle\sum_{m=1}^{\infty}\big(

\displaystyle F^{N}(x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{N})-F^{N}(x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m-1}\wedge t}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m-1}\wedge t}^{N})\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{m=1}^{\infty}\Big\{\sum_{u=1}^{d}\int_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m-1}\wedge t}^{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}\partial_{x_{u}}F^{N}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{s}^{N})b^{u}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})ds

\displaystyle-\sum_{u=1}^{d}\int_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m-1}\wedge t}^{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}\int_{Z}\partial_{x_{u}}F^{N}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{s}^{N})\gamma^{u}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},z)\nu(dz)ds

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{N}\sum_{u=1}^{d}\int_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m-1}\wedge t}^{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}\partial_{x^{k}_{u}}F^{N}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{s}^{N})b^{u}(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})ds

\displaystyle-\sum_{k=1}^{N}\sum_{u=1}^{d}\int_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m-1}\wedge t}^{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}\int_{Z}\partial_{x^{k}_{u}}F^{N}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{s}^{N})\gamma^{u}(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},z)\nu(dz)ds

\displaystyle+\sum_{u=1}^{d}\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\int_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m-1}\wedge t}^{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}\partial_{x_{u}}F^{N}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{s}^{N})\sigma^{u\ell}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})dw_{s}^{i\ell}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{N}\sum_{u=1}^{d}\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\int_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m-1}\wedge t}^{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}\partial_{x^{k}_{u}}F^{N}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{s}^{N})\sigma^{u\ell}(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})dw_{s}^{k\ell}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{u,v=1}^{d}\int_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m-1}\wedge t}^{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}\partial_{x_{u}}\partial_{x_{v}}F^{N}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{s}^{N})\sigma^{(u)}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})\sigma^{(v)}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})ds

\displaystyle+\sum_{u,v=1}^{d}\int_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m-1}\wedge t}^{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}\partial_{x_{u}}\partial_{x^{i}_{v}}F^{N}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{s}^{N})\sigma^{(u)}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})\sigma^{(v)}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})ds

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{k=1}^{N}\sum_{u,v=1}^{d}\int_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m-1}\wedge t}^{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}\partial_{x^{k}_{u}}\partial_{x^{k}_{v}}F^{N}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{s}^{N})\sigma^{(u)}(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})\sigma^{(v)}(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})ds\Big\}

almost surely for any
t\in[0,T]
and
i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
which can be rewritten in the following form

\displaystyle\sum_{m=1}^{\infty}\big(F^{N}(x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{N})-F^{N}(x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m-1}\wedge t}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m-1}\wedge t}^{N})\big)=\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{x}F^{N}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{s}^{N})b(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})ds

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Z}\partial_{x}F^{N}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{s}^{N})\gamma(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},z)\nu(dz)ds+\sum_{k=1}^{N}\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{x^{k}}F^{N}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{s}^{N})b(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})ds

\displaystyle-\sum_{k=1}^{N}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Z}\partial_{x^{k}}F^{N}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{s}^{N})\gamma(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},z)\nu(dz)ds+\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{x}F^{N}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{s}^{N})\sigma^{\ell}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})dw_{s}^{i\ell}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\sum_{k=1}^{N}\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{x^{k}}F^{N}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{s}^{N})\sigma^{\ell}(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})dw_{s}^{k\ell}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\tr[\partial_{x}^{2}F^{N}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{s}^{N})\sigma(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})\sigma^{*}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})]ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\tr[\partial_{x}\partial_{x^{i}}F^{N}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{s}^{N})\sigma(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})\sigma^{*}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})]ds

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{k=1}^{N}\int_{0}^{t}\tr[\partial_{x^{k}}^{2}F^{N}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{s}^{N})\sigma(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})\sigma^{*}(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})]ds

almost surely for any
t\in[0,T]
and

.
For expressing terms on the right side of the above equation in Lions derivatives, we make use of Proposition 5.35 and Proposition 5.91 from

and write

\displaystyle\partial_{x^{k}}F^{N}(x,x^{1},\ldots,x^{N})=\frac{1}{N}\partial_{\mu}F\Big(x,\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\delta_{x^{j}},x^{k}\Big),

\displaystyle\partial_{x^{k}}^{2}F^{N}(x,x^{1},\ldots,x^{N})=\frac{1}{N}\partial_{y}\partial_{\mu}F\Big(x,\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\delta_{x^{j}},x^{k}\Big)+\frac{1}{N^{2}}\partial_{\mu}^{2}F\Big(x,\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\delta_{x^{j}},x^{k},x^{k}\Big)

,

Then, one obtains

\displaystyle\sum_{m=1}^{\infty}\big(F^{N}(x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{i,N},

\displaystyle\mathcal{X}_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{N})-F^{N}(x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m-1}\wedge t}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m-1}\wedge t}^{N})\big)=\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{x}F(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})b(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})ds

\displaystyle\quad-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Z}\partial_{x}F(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})\gamma(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},z)\nu(dz)ds

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{\mu}F(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},x_{s}^{k,N})b(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})ds

\displaystyle+\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{x}F(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})\sigma^{\ell}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})dw_{s}^{i\ell}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\sum_{k=1}^{N}\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{\mu}F(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},x_{s}^{k,N})\sigma^{\ell}(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})dw_{s}^{k\ell}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\tr[\partial_{x}^{2}F(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})\sigma(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})\sigma^{*}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})]ds

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N}\int_{0}^{t}\tr[\partial_{x}\partial_{\mu}F(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},x_{s}^{i,N})\sigma(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})\sigma^{*}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})]ds

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}\int_{0}^{t}\tr[\partial_{y}\partial_{\mu}F(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},x_{s}^{k,N})\sigma(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})\sigma^{*}(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})]ds

(67)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2N^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{N}\int_{0}^{t}\tr[\partial_{\mu}^{2}F(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},x_{s}^{k,N},x_{s}^{k,N})\sigma(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})\sigma^{*}(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})]ds

almost surely for any

and

Now, the second term on the right side of Equation (

\displaystyle=\sum_{m=1}^{\infty}\Big\{F^{N}\Big(x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}^{i,N},x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}^{1,N},x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}^{2,N},\ldots,x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}^{N,N}\Big)-F^{N}\Big(x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}^{i,N},x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{1,N},x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}^{2,N},\ldots,x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}^{N,N}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-F^{N}\Big(x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}^{i,N},x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{1,N},\ldots,x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{i-1,N},x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}^{i,N},\ldots,x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}^{N,N}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-F^{N}\Big(x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{i,N},x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{1,N},\ldots,x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{i+1,N},x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}^{i+2,N},\ldots,x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}^{N,N}\Big)

\displaystyle+\cdots+F^{N}\Big(x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{i,N},x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{1,N},x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{2,N},\ldots,x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{N,N},x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}^{N,N}\Big)-F^{N}\Big(x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{i,N},x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{1,N},x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{2,N},\ldots,x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{N,N}\Big)\Big\}

which on using the fact that particles do not jump together yields

\displaystyle\sum_{m=1}^{\infty}\big(F^{N}(x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}^{N})-F^{N}(x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{N})\big)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t+}\int_{Z}\Big\{F^{N}\big(x_{s}^{i,N},x_{s-}^{1,N}+\gamma(x_{s}^{1,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},z),x_{s}^{2,N},\ldots,x_{s}^{N,N}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-F^{N}\big(x_{s}^{i,N},x_{s-}^{1,N},x_{s}^{2,N},\ldots,x_{s}^{N,N}\big)\Big\}n_{p}^{1}(ds,dz)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t+}\int_{Z}\Big\{F^{N}\big(x_{s}^{i,N},x_{s-}^{1,N},x_{s-}^{2,N}+\gamma(x_{s}^{2,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},z),\ldots,x_{s}^{N,N}\big)

\displaystyle+\cdots+\int_{0}^{t+}\int_{Z}\Big\{F^{N}\big(x_{s}^{i,N},x_{s-}^{1,N},\ldots,x_{s-}^{i-2,N},x_{s-}^{i-1,N}+\gamma(x_{s}^{i-1,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},z),x_{s}^{i,N},\ldots,x_{s}^{N,N}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-F^{N}\big(x_{s}^{i,N},x_{s-}^{1,N},\ldots,x_{s-}^{i-1,N},x_{s}^{i,N},\ldots,x_{s}^{N,N}\big)\Big\}n_{p}^{i-1}(ds,dz)+\cdots

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t+}\int_{Z}\Big\{F^{N}(x_{s-}^{i,N}+\gamma(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},z),x_{s-}^{1,N},\ldots,x_{s-}^{i-1,N},x_{s-}^{i,N}+\gamma(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},z),x_{s}^{i+1,N},\ldots,x_{s}^{N,N})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-F^{N}(x_{s-}^{i,N},x_{s-}^{1,N},\ldots,x_{s-}^{i,N},x_{s}^{i+1,N},\ldots,x_{s}^{N,N})\Big\}n_{p}^{i}(ds,dz)+\cdots

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t+}\int_{Z}\Big\{F^{N}(x_{s-}^{i,N},x_{s-}^{1,N},\ldots,x_{s-}^{i,N},x_{s-}^{i+1,N}+\gamma(x_{s}^{i+1,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},z),x_{s}^{i+2,N},\ldots,x_{s}^{N,N})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-F^{N}(x_{s-}^{i,N},x_{s-}^{1,N},\ldots,x_{s-}^{i+1,N},x_{s}^{i+2,N},\ldots,x_{s}^{N,N})\Big\}n_{p}^{i+1}(ds,dz)+\cdots

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t+}\int_{Z}\Big\{F^{N}(x_{s-}^{i,N},x_{s-}^{1,N},\ldots,x_{s-}^{N-1,N},x_{s-}^{N,N}+\gamma(x_{s}^{N,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},z))

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-F^{N}(x_{s-}^{i,N},x_{s-}^{1,N},\ldots,x_{s-}^{N,N})\Big\}n_{p}^{N}(ds,dz)

almost surely for any
t\in[0,T]
and
i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
.
Further, the above can be expressed as follows

\displaystyle\sum_{m=1}^{\infty}

\displaystyle\big(F^{N}(x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t}^{N})-F^{N}(x_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{i,N},\mathcal{X}_{\widetilde{\tau}_{m}\wedge t-}^{N})\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{N}\hskip-1.42271pt\int_{0}^{t+}\hskip-2.84544pt\int_{Z}\hskip-1.42271pt\Big\{F^{N}\big(x_{s-}^{i,N}\hskip-0.71114pt+\hskip-0.71114pt1_{\{k=i\}}\gamma(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},z),x_{s-}^{1,N},\ldots,x_{s-}^{k,N}\hskip-0.71114pt+\hskip-0.71114pt\gamma(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},z),x_{s}^{k+1,N},\ldots,x_{s}^{N,N}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-F^{N}\big(x_{s-}^{i,N},x_{s-}^{1,N},\ldots,x_{s-}^{k,N},x_{s}^{k+1,N},\ldots,x_{s}^{N,N}\big)\Big\}n_{p}^{k}(ds,dz)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{N}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Z}\Big\{F\Big(x_{s-}^{i,N}+1_{\{k=i\}}\gamma(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},z),\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\delta_{x_{s-}^{j,N}+1_{\{j=k\}}\gamma(x_{s}^{j,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},z)}\Big)

(68)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-F\Big(x_{s-}^{i,N},\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\delta_{x_{s-}^{j,N}}\Big)\Big\}n_{p}^{k}(ds,dz)

almost surely for any
t\in[0,T]
and
i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
.
By substituting Equations (

) and (
68
) in Equation (

), we get

\displaystyle F(x_{t}^{i,N},\,\mu_{t}^{x,N})=F(x_{0}^{i,N},\mu_{0}^{x,N})

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{x}F(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})b(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})ds-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Z}\partial_{x}F(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})\gamma(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},z)\nu(dz)ds

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{\mu}F(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},x_{s}^{k,N})b(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})ds

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Z}\partial_{\mu}F(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},x_{s}^{k,N})\gamma(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},z)\nu(dz)ds

\displaystyle+\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{x}F(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})\sigma^{\ell}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})dw_{s}^{i\ell}+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\sum_{k=1}^{N}\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{\mu}F(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},x_{s}^{k,N})\sigma^{\ell}(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})dw_{s}^{k\ell}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\tr[\partial_{x}^{2}F(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})\sigma(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})\sigma^{*}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})]ds

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N}\int_{0}^{t}\tr[\partial_{x}\partial_{\mu}F(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},x_{s}^{i,N})\sigma(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})\sigma^{*}(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})]ds

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}\int_{0}^{t}\tr[\partial_{y}\partial_{\mu}F(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},x_{s}^{k,N})\sigma(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})\sigma^{*}(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})]ds

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2N^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{N}\int_{0}^{t}\tr[\partial_{\mu}^{2}F(x_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},x_{s}^{k,N},x_{s}^{k,N})\sigma(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})\sigma^{*}(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N})]ds

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{N}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{Z}\Big\{F\big(x_{s-}^{i,N}+1_{\{k=i\}}\gamma(x_{s}^{k,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},z),\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\delta_{x_{s-}^{j,N}+1_{\{j=k\}}\gamma(x_{s}^{j,N},\mu_{s}^{x,N},z)})

(69)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-F(x_{s-}^{i,N},\mu_{s-}^{x,N}\big)\Big\}n_{p}^{k}(ds,dz)

almost surely for any
t\in[0,T]
and
i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
2.2
.

Let

f(x)=|x|^{p}

for any
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
.
Then, for any
u,v\in\{1,\ldots,d\}
and
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,

\displaystyle\partial_{x^{u}}f(x)=p|x|^{p-2}x^{u}\mbox{ and }\partial_{x^{v}}\partial_{x^{u}}f(x)=p(p-2)|x|^{p-4}x^{u}x^{v}+p|x|^{p-2}1_{\{u=v\}}.

By Taylor’s theorem for a
f\in\mathcal{C}^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})

\displaystyle f(x)-f(y)-\sum_{u=1}^{d}(x^{u}-y^{u})\partial_{x^{u}}f(y)=\sum_{u,v=1}^{d}(x^{u}-y^{u})(x^{v}-y^{v})\int_{0}^{1}(1-\theta)\partial_{x^{v}}\partial_{x^{u}}f\big(y+\theta(x-y)\big)d\theta

which on taking
f(x)=|x|^{p}
yields

\displaystyle|x|^{p}-|y|^{p}-p\sum_{u=1}^{d}(x^{u}-y^{u})|y|^{p-2}y^{u}

\displaystyle=p(p-2)\sum_{u,v=1}^{d}(x^{u}-y^{u})(x^{v}-y^{v})\int_{0}^{1}(1-\theta)\big|y+\theta(x-y)\big|^{p-4}\big(y^{u}+\theta(x^{u}-y^{u})\big)\big(y^{v}+\theta(x^{v}-y^{v})\big)d\theta

\displaystyle\quad+p\sum_{u,v=1}^{d}(x^{u}-y^{u})(x^{v}-y^{v})\int_{0}^{1}(1-\theta)\big|y+\theta(x-y)\big|^{p-2}1_{\{u=v\}}d\theta

\displaystyle=p(p-2)\int_{0}^{1}(1-\theta)\big|y+\theta(x-y)\big|^{p-4}\Big(\sum_{u=1}^{d}(x^{u}-y^{u})\big(y^{u}+\theta(x^{u}-y^{u})\Big)^{2}d\theta

\displaystyle\quad+p\sum_{u=1}^{d}(x^{u}-y^{u})^{2}\int_{0}^{1}(1-\theta)\big|y+\theta(x-y)\big|^{p-2}d\theta

\displaystyle\leq p(p-2)\int_{0}^{1}(1-\theta)\big|y+\theta(x-y)\big|^{p-4}|x-y|^{2}\big|y+\theta(x-y)\big|^{2}d\theta

\displaystyle\quad+p|x-y|^{2}\int_{0}^{1}(1-\theta)\big|y+\theta(x-y)\big|^{p-2}d\theta=p(p-1)|x-y|^{2}\int_{0}^{1}(1-\theta)\big|y+\theta(x-y)\big|^{p-2}d\theta

for any
x,y\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
.
∎
